- **ylj-workflow**: Use as the entry point for any The Yale Law Journal (YLJ) piece. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Yale-Law-Journal-Skills/skills/ylj-writing-style) · ★1.1k)
